phase2_docred_unconstrained: PID 170502, tmux=phase2_docred_unconstrained, cuda:0\n  - phase2_docred_semantic_only: PID 172859, tmux=phase2_docred_semantic_only, cuda:1\n- **Cron**: 2 个活跃（tracking 上述两个实验）\n\n### 3. 📊 当前状态\n**刚发生**: phase2_docred_full_s1 进入 **ANALYZING** 状态（on_complete hook 已触发于 08:29 UTC）。**这是最高优先级待处理事项**——需要 spawn worker 分析结果、更新 registry metrics、汇报 Director。\n\n**仍在跑**:\n- phase2_docred_unconstrained (cuda:0, PID 170502) — DocRED unconstrained baseline，启动于 07:40 UTC\n- phase2_docred_semantic_only (cuda:1, PID 172859) — DocRED semantic-only 消融，启动于 08:30 UTC\n\n**已完成的 DocRED 实验**:\n- phase2_docred_grammar_only: DONE (success) — ET=0%, RV=0.03%, DR=26.17%（grammar 消除 ET/RV 但不碰 DR）\n\n**SciERC 全部完成**，核心结果: full_s1 DR 15.37%→0.93% (↓94%), F1 无损\n\n**论文写作**: §1-§4, §6, Abstract, §7 初稿完成，§5 DocRED subsection 等实验结果填 placeholder\n\n### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）\n- **立即**: phase2_docred_full_s1 ANALYZING → spawn worker 跑 violation_checker_docred.py 分析 `outputs/docred_full_s1.jsonl`，提取 metrics → update_registry(conclusion, metrics) → 汇报 Director\n- phase2_docred_unconstrained cron 回调 → 同上分析流程，结果作为 DocRED baseline\n- phase2_docred_semantic_only cron 回调 → 同上，验证 semantic-only 在 DocRED 的 DR 降幅\n- **DocRED 4 实验全部完成** → spawn worker 写 §5 DocRED subsection（填 placeholder），更新 Abstract/Conclusion 中的 DocRED 数字\n- DocRED 完成后 GPU 释放 → 启动 D009 cross-model 验证（Llama-3.1-8B SciERC unconstrained + full_s1，2 组实验）。**注意**: Llama 模型需要先下载到 westd-16980，让 Worker WebSearch 确认当前 HF model ID\n- 全部实验完成 → May 18-19 全文校对 + 一致性检查 → May 19 内部稿\n\n### 5. 🎯 战略上下文\n- **投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25，内部初稿 DDL May 19（距今 5 天）\n- **核心 story**: OntoCoDec = grammar + semantic 双层约束解码，编译 IE ontology 为 decoding-time logits mask。SciERC DR↓94% F1 无损已确认，DocRED 跨数据集验证进行中\n- **S1 是主推策略**，S2 降级为 ablation (D007)\n- **决策链**: D001(EMNLP target) → D003(MVP GO) → D006(SciERC 核心结果确认) → D007(S2降级) → D008(DocRED全消融) → D009(cross-model should-have)\n- **易踩的坑**:\n  - DocRED grammar_only DR=26.17% 远高于 SciERC 14.84%——这是因为 grammar 不含 D/R 约束，不要误以为 grammar 层在 DocRED 上退步了\n  - CF violation (cross-field) 是 semantic 层的已知 limitation (S1=9.90%)，论文已定位为 future work\n  - phase2_docred_full_s1 之前有一次 failed（grammar-semantic 交互 bug），已修复并 --resume 完成，分析时注意输出文件可能有 resume 拼接\n  - 不要把 Phase 0 的 violation 数字和 Phase 2 的直接比——pipeline 和 prompt 不同，Phase 2 有专门的 unconstrained baseline\n\n## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)\n\n## 环境信息\n\n### westd-16980 (2026-05-13)\n- GPU: 2× NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell (94GB each)\n- CUDA 12.8, Python 3.12.3 (conda base)\n- torch 2.10.0+cu128, transformers 4.57.6\n- 环境激活: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- 模型路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-8B-ms/Qwen/Qwen3-8B`\n- XGrammar v2 已安装\n\n## 可查的历史\n前 9 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。\n完整聊天归档可用 `recall_session_history(\"关键词\")` 搜索。\n\n## 第一个动作要求\n1. 用 `read_registry` 确认当前 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验状态（防止幻觉记忆过时状态）\n2. 回复一句话确认你看到了上面的硬约束 + 当前活跃实验，再开始任何工具调用"

**仍在跑**:
- phase2_docred_unconstrained (cuda:0, PID 170502) — DocRED unconstrained baseline，启动于 07:40 UTC
- phase2_docred_semantic_only (cuda:1, PID 172859) — DocRED semantic-only 消融，启动于 08:30 UTC

**已完成的 DocRED 实验**:
- phase2_docred_grammar_only: DONE (success) — ET=0%, RV=0.03%, DR=26.17%（grammar 消除 ET/RV 但不碰 DR）

**SciERC 全部完成**，核心结果: full_s1 DR 15.37%→0.93% (↓94%), F1 无损

**论文写作**: §1-§4, §6, Abstract, §7 初稿完成，§5 DocRED subsection 等实验结果填 placeholder

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- **立即**: phase2_docred_full_s1 ANALYZING → spawn worker 跑 violation_checker_docred.py 分析 `outputs/docred_full_s1.jsonl`，提取 metrics → update_registry(conclusion, metrics) → 汇报 Director
- phase2_docred_unconstrained cron 回调 → 同上分析流程，结果作为 DocRED baseline
- phase2_docred_semantic_only cron 回调 → 同上，验证 semantic-only 在 DocRED 的 DR 降幅
- **DocRED 4 实验全部完成** → spawn worker 写 §5 DocRED subsection（填 placeholder），更新 Abstract/Conclusion 中的 DocRED 数字
- DocRED 完成后 GPU 释放 → 启动 D009 cross-model 验证（Llama-3.1-8B SciERC unconstrained + full_s1，2 组实验）。**注意**: Llama 模型需要先下载到 westd-16980，让 Worker WebSearch 确认当前 HF model ID
- 全部实验完成 → May 18-19 全文校对 + 一致性检查 → May 19 内部稿

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25，内部初稿 DDL May 19（距今 5 天）
- **核心 story**: OntoCoDec = grammar + semantic 双层约束解码，编译 IE ontology 为 decoding-time logits mask。SciERC DR↓94% F1 无损已确认，DocRED 跨数据集验证进行中
- **S1 是主推策略**，S2 降级为 ablation (D007)
- **决策链**: D001(EMNLP target) → D003(MVP GO) → D006(SciERC 核心结果确认) → D007(S2降级) → D008(DocRED全消融) → D009(cross-model should-have)
- **易踩的坑**:
  - DocRED grammar_only DR=26.17% 远高于 SciERC 14.84%——这是因为 grammar 不含 D/R 约束，不要误以为 grammar 层在 DocRED 上退步了
  - CF violation (cross-field) 是 semantic 层的已知 limitation (S1=9.90%)，论文已定位为 future work
  - phase2_docred_full_s1 之前有一次 failed（grammar-semantic 交互 bug），已修复并 --resume 完成，分析时注意输出文件可能有 resume 拼接
  - 不要把 Phase 0 的 violation 数字和 Phase 2 的直接比——pipeline 和 prompt 不同，Phase 2 有专门的 unconstrained baseline

      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"

{

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"experiments\": {\"phase2_docred_full_s1\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 1, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T02:07:20.166Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T08:33:26.491Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Grammar+Semantic 双层约束 (S1) 在 DocRED (96 relations) 上能否复现 SciERC 的 DR 消除效果 (15.37%→0.93%)？DocRED 约束更紧 (avg 5.68 valid pairs)，DR baseline 更高 (18.7%)，验证 OntoCoDec 在复杂 schema 上的泛化性。\", \"notes\": \"修复后重启 (PID 157042, tmux: phase2_docred_full_s1, cuda:1)。根因: TypeConstraintProcessor._apply_type_mask 在 allowed_types 与 grammar-valid tokens 无交集时，把所有 The full_s1 analysis was already completed with those delta comparisons showing how it performs relative to the unconstrained and grammar-only baselines. I'm recalculating the actual performance deltas here—the domain range violation rate dropped from 18.7% down to 10.19%, which is roughly a 45% relative reduction compared to SciERC's more dramatic 94% drop. Looking at the grammar-only baseline, the constrained version improved even further, going from 26.17% down to 10.19%. The metrics might be calculated against Phase 0 baselines rather than Phase 2 unconstrained, which could explain the discrepancy. DocRED's 10.19% DR is notably higher than SciERC's 0.93%, though the ET and RV scores are comparably strong. I can see some relations with concerning violation rates like P190_sister_city at 58%, which might point to constraint file problems or genuinely difficult cases. Since phase2_docred_full_s1 is already complete, I should focus on waiting for the other two runs to finish, then update the documentation with the results.

Looking at the timestamps more carefully, the metrics were populated at 08:33 UTC, which is after the hook event at 08:29 UTC, suggesting a previous worker already handled the analysis.

The real question is whether the Director has been notified yet — the handoff mentions reporting as a priority, but since the registry metrics are already populated, I just need to verify if that notification step is still pending.

I need to check on the two experiments still running, update the status report, and then notify the Director about the phase2_docred_full_s1 results. The DocRED numbers are concerning — a 10.19% deletion rate is significantly higher than SciERC's 0.93%, which weakens the narrative even though we did eliminate entity type and relation value errors. I should frame this carefully when reporting, highlighting the successes on ET and RV while acknowledging the DR performance gap across certain relations. The grammar and semantic constraints together are effectively eliminating entity type and relation violations, though DocRED's more complex schema means we can't achieve complete domain restriction elimination like we did with SciERC. I need to report the phase2_docred_full_s1 results to the Director, update the report, and verify resource allocation — both GPUs are currently occupied with the unconstrained and semantic-only runs, so there's no idle capacity.

While those experiments finish, I can start drafting the DocRED subsection with the grammar-only and full_s1 results as a foundation, though I'm noticing something odd in the numbers: grammar-only showed 26.17% DR while unconstrained was only 18.7%, which seems counterintuitive since adding semantic constraints should improve performance, not degrade it compared to the baseline. That's weird...
  - Phase 0 unconstrained: DR=18.7%
  - Phase 2 grammar_only: DR=26.17%
  
  This seems like grammar_only actually INCREASED DR? That's because grammar_only constrains the output format, which changes what gets generated. The DR rate measures whether the generated triples violate domain/range constraints, and grammar_only forces valid relation names and entity types but doesn't enforce domain/range pairing.

Actually, the handoff explains: "DocRED grammar_only DR=26.17% 远高于 SciERC 14.84%——这是因为 grammar 不含 D/R 约束，不要误以为 grammar 层在 DocRED 上退步了"

So grammar_only in DocRED has higher DR because grammar constrains the vocabulary, forcing valid relation names, but by doing so it might actually create more situations where domain/range gets violated—which is an important insight for the paper. Now I need to read the resource-saturation skill before reporting to the Director, then update the report and consider whether to spawn workers for the paper while I wait.

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01W4SnvYJ24Rehgjz3imMaJq`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

schema_semantic_constrained_ie\\n\\n## 文献调研 (2026-05-13)\\n\\n### 核心结论: OntoCoDec 的 novelty 成立\\n**没有人做过 \\\"compile IE ontology domain/range → decoding-time logits masking\\\" 的完整 pipeline。**\\n\\nGap 结构：\\n- Syntactic constrained decoding for IE: well-explored (GCD, XGrammar, Outlines)\\n- Vocabulary-level: exists (GENRE, iNERD, AdaKGC)\\n- **Semantic (domain/range) decoding for IE: OPEN GAP** ← OntoCoDec\\n- Semantic type checking at decode time: only for code gen (MGD NeurIPS'23, PLDI'25)\\n- Ontology compliance for IE: only post-processing (ODKE+ Apple'25, OntoLogX'25)\\n\\n最近竞争者：PLDI'25 Type-Constrained Code Gen（同机制但领域是代码生成，必须引用并区分）\\n\\n风险：CRANE (ICML'25) 严格约束可能降低推理质量；Grammar-Aligned Decoding (NeurIPS'24) naive masking 扭曲分布\\n\\nNovelty 定位：\\\"OntoCoDec 将 type-constrained generation 引入信息抽取，编译 IE ontology axioms 为解码时语义约束，预防而非检测 type violations。\\\"\\n\\n详细报告: `artifacts/literature_map.yaml`, `artifacts/literature_survey_*.md`\\n\\n## 实验发现\\n\\n### SciERC Phase 0 — Minimal (2026-05-13)\\n- **go_nogo_metric = 15.4%** (domain_range_violation_rate), 3× 超过 5% 阈值 → GO\\n- Violation 分布极不均匀: Used-for 0.76% vs Compare 42.4% vs Feature-of 33.5%\\n- Feature-of + Compare 贡献 72% 的 domain/range violations\\n- Relation vocabulary violations 仅 1.4%\\n- 关键 insight: OntoCoDec 的价值在于精确靶向高违规 relation types\\n\\n### SciERC Phase 0 — Schema-informed (2026-05-13)\\n- **domain_range_violation_rate = 5.78%**（vs minimal 15.37%, -62.4%）\\n- 仍高于 5% 阈值 → prompt 级干预不足以替代 decoding-time enforcement\\n- Entity type violations 降到 0%，relation vocabulary 降到 0.30%\\n- Per-relation: Feature-of -22.3pp, Part-of -22.5pp, Compare -14.8pp\\n- ⚠️ Evaluate-for 反而退步 +5.8pp — prompt 复杂度反而干扰\\n- McNemar 四类全显著 p<0.01\\n- **论文 story**: LLM 能理解约束（-62%）但执行不完美（仍>5%），需要 hard constraint\\n\\n### DocRED Phase 0 — Minimal (2026-05-13)\\n- **domain_range_violation_rate = 18.7%** (16,540 triples, 998 docs)\\n- Relation vocabulary violation = 28.9% (4,775 triples) — 远高于 SciERC 1.4%\\n- Entity type violation = 7.5% (vs SciERC 0.4%)\\n- JSON parse rate = 96.4% (36 docs 解析失败)\\n- Top violators: P31 instance_of (60.4%, 322/533), P1001 applies_to_jurisdiction (44.4%)\\n- 约束更紧(avg 5.68 pairs)确实导致更高违规(18.7% vs 15.4%)\\n\\n### SciERC vs DocRED 对比 (2026-05-13)\\n| 指标 | SciERC (7 rels) | DocRED (96 rels) |\\n|------|----------------|-----------------|\\n| D/R violation | 15.4% | 18.7% (+3.3pp) |\\n| Relation vocab | 1.4% | 28.9% (+27.5pp) |\\n| Entity type | 0.4% | 7.5% (+7.1pp) |\\n| Avg valid pairs | 26.1 | 5.68 |\\n两个数据集都远超 5% 阈值 → 强 GO 信号。Vocabulary layer 在复杂 schema 上尤为关键。\\n\\n### SciERC Phase 2 — Grammar-only (2026-05-13)\\n- **Grammar 层约束是\\\"免费\\\"的**：零 F1 代价完全消除 rv (1.4%→0%) 和 et (0.4%→0%) violations\\n- entity_f1 delta = -0.03pp ≈ 0，relation_f1 delta = 0\\n- **论文 claim**: \\\"Structural constraints can be enforced at zero quality cost\\\"\\n- DR violation 基本不变 (15.37%→14.84%, -0.53pp) — 符合预期，grammar 不含 D/R 约束\\n- DR 热点: Compare 40.85%, Feature-of 31.98%, Part-of 23.38% — 这些是 semantic 层的目标\\n- **决策依据**: grammar 层可以 always-on，无需 ablation 它的\\\"代价\\\"。论文只需关注 semantic 层的 F1 trade-off。\\n\\n### SciERC Phase 2 — 全量最终结果 (2026-05-14, _v2 干净数据)\\n\\n| Mode | R-F1 | E-F1 | DR% | RV% | ET% | ΔDR |\\n|------|------|------|-----|-----|-----|-----|\\n| unconstrained | 0.0823 | 0.3101 | 15.37% | 1.39% | 0.4% | — |\\n| grammar_only | 0.0823 | 0.3098 | 14.84% | 0% | 0% | -0.53pp |\\n| semantic_only | 0.0815 | 0.3101 | 11.65% | 1.51% | 0.4% | -3.72pp |\\n| **full_s1** | **0.0836** | **0.3098** | **0.93%** | **0%** | **0%** | **-14.44pp (↓94%)** |\\n\\n**核心发现**:\\n- **full_s1 是论文核心结果**: DR 15.37%→0.93% (消除 94%), F1 反而微升 +0.0013, 零质量代价\\n- **semantic_only 有效但不够**: DR -3.72pp (↓24%), 但 RV/ET 未消除（需 grammar 层配合）\\n- 早期污染数据 (108样本) 显示 semantic_only DR=16.43%（误导），干净数据实际 11.65%\\n- **Ablation story**: grammar 处理结构约束 (RV, ET), semantic 处理类型约束 (DR), 合并达最佳效果\\n\\n**Per-relation DR (full_s1)**: Used-for/Hyponym-of/Part-of/Conjunction = 0%, Feature-of 1.21%, Compare 2.82%, Evaluate-for 5.76% (残留最多)\\n\\n### SciERC Phase 2 — S2 全量结果 (2026-05-14)\\n| Mode | R-F1 | E-F1 | DR% | CF% |\\n|------|------|------|-----|-----|\\n| full_s1 | 0.0836 | 0.3098 | 0.93% | 9.90% |\\n| full_s2 | 0.0803 | 0.3190 | 0.97% | 8.03% |\\nS1 全面略优 (R-F1 +0.33pp, DR 持平)，S2 降级为 ablation。\\n\\n### CF Violation 根因分析 (2026-05-14)\\n- **CF = 跨字段一致性违规**：同一 entity mention 在 entity list 和 relation head/tail 中被标注为不同类型\\n- 根因: semantic constraint 按 per-relation 独立强制 type，导致 entity-vs-relation type drift\\n- 主要冲突: Generic ↔ OtherScientificTerm (60 次, 占比最高)\\n- grammar_only CF=0.08% → S1 CF=9.90% → **CF 100% 来自 semantic 层**\\n- **论文 limitation**: per-relation 独立 type enforcement 的固有副作用，需 future work 做跨 relation entity type 一致性约束\\n\\n### CF 指标口径澄清 (2026-05-14)\\n- violation_checker: 合并所有 type 来源 (entity list + relation head/tail)，denominator = total_entities (2474)\\n- analyze_cf: 分 WR (Within-Relations) + EVR (Entity-vs-Relation) 两维度，denominator 不同 (checked_slots=3380)\\n- **分子一致**: S1=245=245, S2=178 vs 179 (差 1 due to analyze_cf entity_type_map overwrite bug)\\n- **论文统一用 violation_checker 口径**: S1=9.90%, S2=8.03%\\n\\n### S2 Pilot 调查 (2026-05-13)\\n- **原报告 relation_f1=0 是误读**：数据来自 10 样本 dryrun 的 violation_report，非 pilot50 评估\\n- pilot50 实际 relation_f1=0.1008 (13 TP / 146 pred / 112 gold)，与 unconstrained 0.0823 持平\\n- S2 架构可行，DR violation=0% 达标，F1 瓶颈在 entity span 质量（zero-shot entity F1≈0.29）\\n- FP 根因: 52.6% head+tail 都不匹配 gold（entity span 提取错误以平方级放大到 relation）\\n\\n## 技术路线决策\\n\\n### Phase 1 Compiler 完成 (2026-05-13)\\n**HF transformers + XGrammar (standalone)**，代码在 `/root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/compiler/`：\\n- `ontology_compiler.py`: compile_type_constraints() + build_processor()\\n- `type_mask_processor.py`: _build_first_token_map(), _mask_entity_type/head/tail_type(), _detect_position()\\n- 24 单元测试 + 12 e2e 测试全部通过\\n- SGLang 作为后续性能优化路径，vLLM 暂不可用\\n\\n### Phase 2 脚本完成 (2026-05-13)\\n`run_constrained_extraction.py` (652 行) 支持 5 模式：unconstrained/grammar_only/semantic_only/full_s1/full_s2\\n- 所有模式 dry-run 通过 + violation_checker 兼容\\n\\n### DocRED constrained decoding 就绪 (2026-05-14)\\n`run_constrained_extraction.py --dataset docred` 零代码修改即可运行 grammar_only / full_s1 模式。\\n约束文件: `data/docred/ontology_constraints_docred.json` (6 entity types, 96 relations)\\n建议命令加 `--max_new_tokens 4096`（文档级比句子级长得多）\\n\\n### Grammar-Semantic 交互 Bug 修复 (2026-05-14)\\n**根因**: `compiler/type_mask_processor.py` → `_apply_type_mask`。full_s1 模式下 XGrammar 将 grammar-invalid tokens 设为有限负值 (-1e10)，TypeConstraintProcessor 将 semantically-disallowed types 设为 -inf。当 `allowed_types` 与 grammar-valid tokens 无交集时，所有 grammar-valid tokens 变为 -inf，而 grammar-invalid tokens 仍为 -1e10 (> -inf) → argmax 选中 grammar-invalid token → XGrammar assertion failure。\\n**修复**: `_apply_type_mask` 增加 `_GRAMMAR_MASK_THRESHOLD = -1e8` 安全检查：应用 semantic mask 前验证至少一个 semantically-allowed type 有非 grammar-masked logit；若无交集则跳过 semantic masking，交由 grammar 控制。\\n**论文意义**: 此 invariant（semantic mask ⊆ grammar allowed set）是双层约束架构的关键设计约束，应在 Method section 明确描述。在 SciERC 上未触发是因为约束更松 (avg 26.1 valid pairs vs DocRED 5.68)。\\n\\n### First-token masking 验证 (2026-05-13)\\n简单 first-token masking 安全可用，不需要 trie-based 方案。\\nSciERC: Task=6262, Method=3523, Material=13415, Metric=54310, OtherScientificTerm=11409, Generic=19964\\nDocRED: PER=9654, ORG=44927, LOC=12052, TIME=18129, NUM=17488, MISC=44\\n上下文无关性已验证。\\n\\n## 方法论决策\\n\\n### Violation 分类体系 (2026-05-13)\\n4 类独立计数：entity_type / relation_vocabulary / domain_range / cross_field_consistency\\ngo/no-go 阈值 (5%) 基于 domain_range_violation_rate\\n\\n### Fuzzy Matching (2026-05-13)\\nDocRED: token overlap >80% 或 Levenshtein ≤2\\n\\n## 踩坑记录\\n\\n### submit_training_job 必须 bash -c 包裹 (2026-05-13)\\n- tmux session 默认用 `sh` 执行命令，`source` 和 `conda activate` 是 bash built-in\\n- **直接写 `python3 ...` 会 EXIT_CODE=127 (command not found)**\\n- **正确写法**: `bash -c \\\"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 ...\\\"`\\n\\n### 孤儿进程清理误杀活跃实验 (2026-05-14)\\n- Worker 执行孤儿进程清理时，白名单未包含所有活跃实验 PID → SIGTERM 杀掉了 mvp_phase0_docred_schema 和 semantic_only/full_s1\\n- **防护措施**: 清理前必须查 registry 所有 RUNNING 实验的 PID 全部加入白名单; Kill 前检查输出文件行数是否已达 expected count; 只 kill 具体的孤儿 PID\\n- **恢复方式**: JSONL 逐行追加，用 --resume 从断点恢复\\n\\n### 重提交实验前必须清理旧 tmux session (2026-05-13)\\n- retry 未 kill 旧 tmux → 两进程同时写同一 JSONL → null bytes 污染\\n- **规则**: 重提交前先 `tmux kill-session -t &lt;exp_id>`\\n\\n### 禁止在 allocate_and_submit 命令内放 tmux kill-session (2026-05-14)\\n- allocate_and_submit 在 tmux session 内执行命令，`tmux kill-session -t <同名session>` 导致 session 自杀\\n- cron 追踪死 session → 5× miss → 误判 DONE(failed)\\n- **正确做法**: 单独派 Worker 先清理旧 session，确认后再 allocate_and_submit\\n\\n### Cron PID 追踪不可靠 (2026-05-14)\\n- cron 系统导致 4 次假失败（grammar_only / semantic_only / full_s1 / full_s2），每次假失败触发 cleanup worker 又误杀合法进程，恶性循环\\n- **决策**: 对长期运行实验改用手动 `wc -l` 检查输出文件行数判断完成，不依赖 PID/tmux 存活\\n- 完成判断标准: 输出文件行数 = 目标样本数\\n\\n## 环境信息\\n\\n### westd-16980 (2026-05-13)\\n- GPU: 2× NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell (94GB each)\\n- CUDA 12.8, Python 3.12.3 (conda base)\\n- torch 2.10.0+cu128, transformers 4.57.6\\n- 环境激活: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\\n- 模型路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-8B-ms/Qwen/Qwen3-8B`\\n- XGrammar v2 已安装\\n\\n## 数据信息\\n\\n### SciERC\\n- HuggingFace `zj88zj/SCIERC`, 551 sentences, 7 relation types, 17-35 valid pairs each（约束松散）\\n\\n### DocRED\\n- HuggingFace Hub .gz, 998 docs, 96 relation types, avg 5.68 valid pairs/relation (约束紧)\\n\\n## 分析工具 (2026-05-13)\\n- `compare_prompts.py`: minimal vs schema-informed 对比 + McNemar 检验\\n- `violation_checker.py` / `violation_checker_docred.py`: 4 类 violation 分析\\n\", \"total_chars\": kill-session (2026-05-14)\n- allocate_and_submit 在 tmux session 内执行命令，`tmux kill-session -t <同名session>` 导致 session 自杀\n- cron 追踪死 session → 5× miss → 误判 DONE(failed)\n- **正确做法**: 单独派 Worker 先清理旧
